Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The data_structures variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 502 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 631 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 502 + O(1/n^631). Summing over all modes and applying the data_structures convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
